ROW	Five-department invariant
Consume exact upstream refs; AUTO/MANUAL changes orchestration only, never canonical source truth. Confirmed work creates a new version. Correction targets exact source version/scope and preserves unaffected content. QA requests correction but does not directly edit upstream output.
P	Five-department invariant
Consume exact upstream refs; AUTO/MANUAL changes orchestration only, never canonical source truth. Confirmed work creates a new version. Correction targets exact source version/scope and preserves unaffected content. QA requests correction but does not directly edit upstream output.

ROW	Construction rule
後續工程若遇到文件未綁定的 exact implementation identity，只能回到 Exact Binding Ledger / Current Authority 補綁，不得在程式或頁面內自行發明第二套 UID、API、Table、Permission、Queue、Provider 或狀態。
P	Construction rule
後續工程若遇到文件未綁定的 exact implementation identity，只能回到 Exact Binding Ledger / Current Authority 補綁，不得在程式或頁面內自行發明第二套 UID、API、Table、Permission、Queue、Provider 或狀態。
P	29. Document Closure / DoD
P	All AI05 owner boundaries are explicit and do not duplicate AI01–AI04 ownership.
P	ASSET / VIDEO / EDIT / VOICE / QA full chain is defined from admission to handoff/release candidate.
P	Layered Composition bounded-change model is normative and testable.
P	Confirmed history is immutable; correction and retry semantics are versioned.
P	QA role is review/request/recheck/gate, never upstream editor or publisher.
P	Cross-stage consumer readiness and invalidation are explicit.
P	All unknown exact implementation bindings are flagged instead of invented.
P	Acceptance, negative regression and Given/When/Then matrices cover normal, conflict, failure and destructive cases.
P	DOCX render/a11y/integrity QA must pass before delivery.
P	Formal governance approval is not self-granted by this document.
ROW	Final document status

P	Final document status
DESIGN_CONTENT_COMPLETE · FORMAL_APPROVAL_NOT_SELF_GRANTED · RUNTIME_NOT_PROVEN_BY_DOCUMENT

P	G. Construction Handoff Rule
P	施工 AI MUST resolve each UI control through this exact order: Page UID → Section/Region → Control UID → Trigger/Operation or Action UID → Gate → Permission → Input/Payload → Method/Path or shared Runtime Operation → Persistence/Provider Owner → Audit/Event → Success/Failure/Recovery → Next Page/Handoff. UI_ONLY/READ_ONLY controls MUST NOT create a write API. System-only operations MUST NOT cause a new visible button unless this closure/source explicitly materializes that control.
P	When a provider/platform/environment value is runtime-specific, the exact binding is the registered Profile/Policy/Environment key named in this annex. Missing required value is a deterministic BLOCK; it is not permission to guess.
P	Unified Page / Control / Action / API / DB / Runtime Construction Matrix
P	[ACPOS-20260921-UNIFIED-CONTROL-RUNTIME-MATRIX] This matrix is the direct construction lookup. Existing source identifiers are preserved. New Action/Gate/Control identities are created only when exhaustive uploaded-source scan found the semantic control/operation but no exact UID/path. Existing exact Method/Path always wins; otherwise effectful operations use POST /v1/commands/{actionUid} through the Shared Typed Command Runtime. UI/read-only controls explicitly bind to N/A or projection runtime and MUST NOT create writes.
